normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options\n__host__ __device__ inline double blackScholes(const option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408,
